2026-10-18T08:11:42.3925152Z   UV_CACHE_DIR: /home/runner/work/_temp/setup-uv-cache
2026-10-18T08:11:42.3930970Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Drop the fused add-RMSNorm + QKV quant from the Firmus max bundle and rerun

Run 39100197595 (23aabcc, Slurm 24503) passed the SiLU, RoPE, fused FP8
KV (with the K-input fix) and Top-K gates, then stopped in
check_add_rmsnorm_group_quant after 84 s, before serving:
('3', 'FP8 codes', 13). For 3 rows the fused kernel's FP8 codes differed
from flashinfer fused_add_rmsnorm + per_token_group_quant in 13 of 12288
elements (limit 0.1% = 12); the scales were identical and every
dequantized value was within one E4M3 step, rows 1 and 2 were bitwise
equal.

13 code flips with identical scales in one 3-row case is far more than
FP32 summation-order noise produces (that flips a BF16 value with
probability ~2^-16 and an FP8 code ~32x less often), so the kernel
differs systematically from the reference somewhere (most likely the
intermediate BF16 rounding of the normed value). Rather than widen the
threshold, drop the patch: its estimated gain was the smallest in the
bundle (Prefill ~1.1-1.6 ms per ~270 ms step, Decode ~2 us per layer)
and its author flagged that it might be slower than flashinfer's
cp.async path. It can return later with a reference-exact reduction.

Changes: remove add-rmsnorm-group-quant.patch from the SGLang chain
(build script, image patch list and its test), its compile in
containers/check-rope-kernel-build.py, its CPU routing check in
check-firmus-latency-source.py and its GPU check in
check-firmus-decode-kernels.py. bench-firmus-sglang-kernels.py already
falls back to the unfused pair when the module is absent. The remaining
22-patch chain applies on fc275674, all five build source checks pass,
pytest and ruff pass.

Everything else is unchanged from 23aabcc. The MegaMoE EP4 gate (14-patch
DeepGEMM series, idle-rank 256-row case) runs for the first time here.
